# H18: Elements kinds

| Field | Value |
|---|---|
| Status | Frozen (elements core, FactSnapshot kind list; elements core 2026-11-02, M1a; FactSnapshot kind list 2026-11-05, M1 exit). Was: Accepted (2026-11-02, M5-P5, plan v10 §6.5 and §7 step 3, orchestrator step AR:32; the review record of every Change-log row has rows 1-4 or gets them in M5-P5 W1, and the Approvals of the dated sets and of this step are written in M5-P5 W1 before the push). Was: Draft (2026-10-21; v0 notes; M5-P1 notes 2026-10-29; M5-P2 notes 2026-10-29). PACKED/HOLEY × INT32/DOUBLE/ELEMENTS are built (M5-P1 ELEM1); DICTIONARY and the allocation-site kind are built (M5-P2 ELEM2); NONEXTENSIBLE, SEALED and FROZEN are not (H18-Q5) |
| Fix by | M1a |
| Freeze point | By part (the Freeze table below, README §3.4; plan v10 V10-A2): "elements core" at M1a; "elements kind values (append-only)" has none; "elements tuning (gated)" at the A4 gate; "FactSnapshot kind list" at M1 exit; "extra element kinds" at M2 exit (M6-PLAN-D6) |
| Origin | v1#14; plan status U |
| Crates | `ep9_vm` (ARRAY and ELEMENTS cells, element paths in `object.rs` and `property.rs`); later `ep9_baseline`, `ep9_opt` (kind checks), `ep9_facts` (elements kind in LiteralFacts, H16) |
| Owner (plan role) | VM lead |
| Validated by | `ep9_vm` array tests; test262 `built-ins/Array` and `language/` array files; later the H16 FactSnapshot elements kind (SP-Facts, M1) |
| Plan sources | [02 §6 H18](../plan/02-architecture.md#6-hard-to-change-decisions-h1-h37), [06 §2.3](../plan/06-object-model-and-spec.md#23-elements-keys-realms-contexts-symbols-h18-unchanged) |

## Context

- Goal: no tag checks in numeric arrays [R06]. JIT and AOT code then load an int32 or a raw f64 with one kind check per array, not one tag check per element.
- G6: a sparse or huge index must not cost memory in proportion to the index.
- Depends on: H6 (hole value 0x22, NaN purification), H8 and H17 (header, the elements slot). Depended on by: H16 (elements kind in feedback and FactSnapshot), H29 (deopt on a kind change), H30 (facts).

## Decision

PACKED/HOLEY × INT32/DOUBLE/ELEMENTS, one-way. DOUBLE stores raw f64 with a hole NaN. Dictionary for sparse arrays (02 §6). Separate kinds for dictionary, frozen/sealed, typed array, arguments and string wrapper (06 §2.3).

```text
 PACKED_INT32 ──> PACKED_DOUBLE ──> PACKED_ELEMENTS
      │                │                  │
      v                v                  v
 HOLEY_INT32  ──> HOLEY_DOUBLE  ──> HOLEY_ELEMENTS ──> DICTIONARY
 (transitions go right or down only; never back)
```

**v0 notes (as built, `dabc38a`; checked again at the P3 merge, `479b1cd`).** v0 has one elements form for every array.

| Item | v0 behavior | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Storage | ARRAY cell plus one ELEMENTS cell of Values; a hole is the H6 empty value 0x22 | `crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:21`, `crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:34`; `object::new_elements` |
| Dense limit | 2^26 elements (`arr::MAX_LENGTH`) | `crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:286-288` |
| Growth | a store at index i makes capacity max(i + 1, 2 × capacity, 4) | `array_set` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:1148`) |
| High dense index | `a[2**26 - 1] = 1` allocates 2^26 slots: 540,606,464 B peak RSS (empty script: 3,473,408 B) | measured 2026-10-21 at `dabc38a`, release `ep9`, M5 Max (not measured again; the growth rule is unchanged) |
| Index at or above 2^26 | stored as a string-keyed property, so the array moves to dictionary mode (an index key never enters a shape, OM-10); `length` does not change (ECMA-262: index + 1) | `crates/ep9_vm/src/property.rs:404-408` falls through |
| `length` above 2^26 | RangeError "Invalid array length" (ECMA-262 allows up to 2^32 - 1) | `property::ordinary_set_walk` at c061b2c (the `length` store; gone in M5-P2, H18-Q4) |
| `delete a[i]` | writes a hole | `crates/ep9_vm/src/property.rs:957-964` |
| Index keys on other objects | string keys (an atom with the index cached; no number keys, W4-NO-NUMBER-KEYS); the first one moves the object to dictionary mode (OM-10) | `shape::add`; test `om10_an_index_key_on_a_non_array_makes_a_dictionary` |
| Element attributes | an accessor on an array index, an element whose attributes are not {w, e, c}, and a non-writable `length` throw "not implemented in ep9 v0" until element storage has attributes; an accessor descriptor for `length` returns false (W4-ACC-ELEM) | `property::define_own_property` |
| Index keys on a prototype | while no prototype has an index key (the sticky `shape::proto_index_keys` flag, H17), `[[Set]]` and `push` add a missing element with no chain walk; after that they walk the chain, so an inherited setter runs (W4-P3FIX-ELEM-FLAG) | `property::array_store` |

**M5-P1 notes (as built, ELEM1, 2026-10-29).** The lattice of the Decision is built except DICTIONARY (kind 6, M5-P2 ELEM2: no object has it yet). So the v0 rows Dense limit, Growth, High dense index, Index at or above 2^26, `length` above 2^26 and Element attributes (for Arrays) still hold. Rows marked "replaces" stand in for the v0 row of that name.

| Item | M5-P1 behavior | Where |
|---|---|---|
| Kind | in the shape: `counts` bits 28-31 (OBJECT_LAYOUT 5). The roots and dictionary shapes of a PROTO_INFO are kept per kind; a kind change installs the shape of the new kind (a prototype gets a new unique shape) | `shape::elements_kind`, `shape::with_elements_kind` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/shape.rs:1667`) |
| Storage (replaces) | kinds 0, 1, 4, 5: Values in an ELEMENTS cell (hole 0x22); kinds 2, 3: raw f64 in an ELEMENTS_DOUBLE cell (kind 81; hole 0xFFF7_FFFF_FFF7_FFFF) that the GC does not read | `crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:35`; `elements::new_double_cell` |
| Store side | the new kind is the join of the old kind and the value's kind (int32: INT32; another number: DOUBLE; anything else: ELEMENTS); a hole, an index past `length` or a growing `length` makes it holey. A change of storage form converts the cell. One-way | `elements::join` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/elements.rs:199`), `elements::transition` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/elements.rs:297`), `object::array_set` |
| Load side | a load reads either form by the cell kind and never changes a kind; a DOUBLE word comes back as `Value::number` makes it (an integral value is an int32 again) | `object::element_at` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:1069`), `object::double_value` |
| NaN | every DOUBLE store writes any NaN as 0x7FF8_0000_0000_0000 (`PURE_NAN_BITS`), so no store writes the hole | `object::double_bits` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:1037`) |
| T0 | `GetKeyedProperty` reads both forms inline; a StoreKeyed hit stores in place only if the value needs no transition (else the cold path makes it) | `interp/ops.rs` `GetKeyedProperty`; `keyed_ic::fast_set` |
| Kinds set (H16, IC_LAYOUT 3) | a keyed slot's value bits 10-16 hold the receiver kinds seen before the access: at every miss, and at a fast hit only in the sampled window (as Call targets); a monomorphic StoreKeyed hit needs none (its shape holds the kind) | `profile::record_keyed_cold`, `profile::keyed_hit_kind` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/profile.rs:889`) |
| Index keys on other objects (replaces) | H18-Q3 below: {w, e, c} index keys at +16, always holey; an int32 key makes the cell, a string index key uses it only once it exists (else a stored property and dictionary mode, OM-10, as in v0) | `elements::plain_elements_kind` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/elements.rs:270`), `object::element_set` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:1263`) |
| Literals | an array literal's cell has the storage form of the kind of its constants; a nested array of a cold object literal gets the kind of its values | `literal_site::alloc_array`, `literal_site::alloc_nested_array` |
| Cost | BENCH-SET instructions, Mac a64, base 6e60ebd, `EP9_HASH_SEED=1`: t0-* at most +0.72%, ci-v0 +0.39%; named: navier-stokes +7.95%, crypto +3.21% (boxing on a DOUBLE load, the cell-kind test per element read), deltablue +1.42%, splay +1.27% | M5-P1 ELEM1 report (V10-D16) |

**M5-P2 notes (as built, ELEM2, 2026-10-29).** DICTIONARY (kind 6) is built for Arrays, so the v0 rows Dense limit, Growth, High dense index, Index at or above 2^26, `length` above 2^26 and Element attributes no longer hold for Arrays: the rows below marked "replaces" stand in for them. Other JS objects keep the M5-P1 H18-Q3 rule (an element they cannot keep leaves for the property storage).

| Item | M5-P2 behavior | Where |
|---|---|---|
| DICTIONARY storage | an ELEMENTS_DICT cell (kind 82, OBJECT_LAYOUT 5): +8 a dense capacity of 0 (so every dense reader, T0 inline paths included, misses at its capacity test), +16 count, +24 used, +32 flags (bit 0: `length` is not writable), +40 buckets (a power of two, 8 or more), then 3 words per entry: the index as an int32 Value, the value or an ACCESSOR_PAIR, the attributes. Open addressing; every word is a Value, so the GC traces them all | `elements::dict` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/elements.rs:459`) |
| Dense limit, Growth (replaces) | 2^26 stays the dense limit. A new element 1,024 or more past the capacity (`elements::ARRAY_GAP`, est., V8's `kMaxGap`), or at or past 2^26, moves the Array to DICTIONARY instead of a dense allocation; nearer ones grow the dense cell as in v0 | `crates/ep9_vm/src/elements.rs:480`; `object::array_set_sparse` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:1187`) |
| Index at or above 2^26, `length` above 2^26 (replaces) | an index up to 2^32 − 2 is an element (DICTIONARY) and sets `length` to index + 1; a `length` up to 2^32 − 1 works. ARRAY +24 holds an int32 Value below 2^31 and a double Value from 2^31 on, so the `length` IC load of +24 gives the right Number; `object::array_len_dense` is the one-load bound of the dense paths | `object::length_value` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/object.rs:994`) |
| Element attributes (replaces) | an element with attributes other than {w, e, c}, an accessor element and a non-writable `length` are DICTIONARY entries and the cell's flag; no "not implemented" throw is left on Arrays. ArraySetLength deletes from the highest index down and stops at a non-configurable entry (`length` = its index + 1, result false); `writable: false` applies after the deletes. A non-writable `length` refuses a new index at or past it | `property::array_define_length` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/property.rs:1830`); the DICTIONARY paths of `property.rs` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/property.rs:549`) |
| Move | one-way (the Decision lattice): the present elements become {w, e, c} entries, then the shape of kind DICTIONARY; no rule moves an Array back to a dense kind | `elements::to_dictionary` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/elements.rs:704`) |
| Storage form | each transition and each DICTIONARY move asserts in debug builds that the cell at +16 is the form of the shape's kind (none or ELEMENTS for 0, 1, 4, 5; none or ELEMENTS_DOUBLE for 2, 3; ELEMENTS_DICT for 6). The shape is the kind source of the tiers (M5-P1-ORCH-11): a monomorphic shape guard fixes the storage form | `elements::storage_form_ok` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/elements.rs:746`) |
| Allocation sites (V10-D6, MODES-D19) | an array-literal site's Literal slot holds an ALLOC_SITE handler (IC_HANDLER kind 8, IC_LAYOUT 3): +16 the site's elements kind (0-5), +24 the result shape (weak), +32 the count; each array made there keeps it at ARRAY +32. The literal cold path makes it and joins the site kind with the result's; a store-side kind transition of such an array joins the site kind with the new kind and sets the result shape. So the site's next arrays are born in the kind its arrays reached (a hit allocates with the result shape), and their stores make no transition. One-way; DICTIONARY is never a birth kind; a hit only reads. No `profile.rs` record hook writes it, so `ics_only` keeps it | `literal_site::array_site` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/literal_site.rs:610`), `literal_site::generalize` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/literal_site.rs:654`) |
| Literals (adds) | an object literal whose nested values are all flat array boilerplates (splay's leaf nodes) is built from its recorded shape by the T0 hit too, not only by the cold path | `literal_site::nested_object` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/literal_site.rs:732`) |
| Facts | `LiteralFacts.elements` is the site's elements kind (the kind its arrays are born with), through the one map `elements::to_fact`; none for an object literal or a site with no handler yet | `facts_export.rs` `literal` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/facts_export.rs:641`) |
| T0 | the StoreKeyed hit has two tails: monomorphic (store, next) and megamorphic (store, then the sampled kinds-set bit), so the monomorphic path pays no budget test (M5-P1-ORCH-3) | `keyed_ic::fast_set` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/keyed_ic.rs:161`) |
| Cost | BENCH-SET instructions, Mac a64, base c061b2c against 47d3b95, `EP9_HASH_SEED=1`, 5 runs after 1 warm-up, medians: t0-* −0.08% to +0.01%, ci-v0 +0.39% (inside the +1.0% budget, V10-D16); named (M5-P1-ORCH-9): navier-stokes −0.06%, crypto −0.30%, splay −12.23% (the nested-literal hit), deltablue +0.36%, raytrace +0.15%; richards +0.01%, earley-boyer −0.02%. Max RSS of every workload within −1.5% to +0.8%, so the V10-D6 fallback (no site cell) is not needed. The ELEM1 waivers of the M5-P1 notes (against 6e60ebd) stand, moved by these amounts | M5-P2 ELEM2 report (V10-D16) |

### Freeze table (README §3.4)

H18 freezes by part (plan v10 V10-A2, V10-D8; README §3.4). At M1a the Frozen step writes `Frozen (elements core; <date>, M1a)`. The parts due at M1 exit join that list when they freeze. A part name holds no `,` and no ` and ` (the Frozen check splits the Status parenthesis there, `tools/xtask/src/exit/freeze.rs:113-125`).

| Part | What it holds | Freeze point | After the freeze point | Check |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| elements core | Every built row of this ADR, except what the parts below hold: the lattice of the Decision over the kinds as built (bit 0 is the holey bit of 0-5; the kind values are in "elements kind values (append-only)"; `crates/ep9_vm/src/elements.rs:89-110`), one-way, and the kind in the shape (`counts` bits 28-31; the H17 layout core holds the position); DOUBLE: the ELEMENTS_DOUBLE cell (81) of raw f64 data, the hole NaN 0xFFF7_FFFF_FFF7_FFFF, and a store writes every NaN as the canonical NaN (H18-Q1); DICTIONARY: the ELEMENTS_DICT cell (82), its entries (the index, the value or an ACCESSOR_PAIR, the attributes) and its flag bit 0 (`length` is not writable) (H18-Q2, Q4); the transitions: the join on stores, one-way, and the storage form follows the shape's kind (`elements::storage_form_ok`); the elements of other objects ({w, e, c} index keys at +16, always holey; H18-Q3); `length` (ARRAY +24: an int32 Value below 2^31, a double Value from 2^31, up to 2^32 − 1); the allocation-site rule: DICTIONARY is never a birth kind, and compiled literal allocation reads the run's ALLOC_SITE handler, so a PROFILED kind is a guard or a hint, never the birth kind (MODES-CHECK-w20, the invariants item I-2); and every Rules row | M1a | Frozen-part rules (README §3.2) | the Rules checks (`tests::elements`, each also under GC stress 1); `presets_object_model` (`crates/ep9_builtins/tests/`); test262 `built-ins/Array` with no new failure; Spike C `vm_layout::check_elem2` on live arrays |
| elements kind values (append-only) | The elements kind values of the shape `counts` bits 28-31 (the H17 layout core holds the position): PACKED_INT32 0, HOLEY_INT32 1, PACKED_DOUBLE 2, HOLEY_DOUBLE 3, PACKED_ELEMENTS 4, HOLEY_ELEMENTS 5, DICTIONARY 6 as built (`elements::kind`). 7-15 are open: a new kind (the "extra element kinds" part, or another kind such as F7 typed storage) takes the next free value; no value is renumbered or reused (M5-P5-ORCH-1, MODES-CHECK-w22 MC-w22-1). Today 7-15 are refused: `elements::storage` and `elements::to_fact` give none, so an object whose shape holds one fails `elements::storage_form_ok`, and the Spike C kind guard exits on them (`vm_layout::ARRAY_KINDS`). [2026-11-05, M6-P4 fixer (B-CHECK F7, F8):] An append that gets a FactSnapshot name names it here too: its `ep9_facts` `ElementsKind` value (the same byte), its `elements::to_fact` arm, its name at the end of the `ElementsKind` and `ElementsKindSet` name lines of `schema.txt` (outside the fingerprint) and its pair in `tests::elements::the_fact_kind_map_is_one_to_one`. Those changes (the "reserved VM kind" case of that test for the new value, and the `schema.txt` golden) are part of the append's fill, as the "not yet used" checks of H17 "representation values (append-only)" are [2026-11-10, TypedArray TA-0 TA-DECL (design `docs/plan/notes/typedarray-design.md` §3.2, Proposal A; TA-D10; M6-ORCH-321):] 7 TYPED_FIXED and 8 TYPED_VARIABLE are appended (`elements::kind`), with the storage form `Typed` (`elements::storage`): a view (a cell of the view kinds 29-40, H17 "view cells") gets 7 or 8 at birth and keeps it, and its +16 is undefined; `elements::storage_form_ok` accepts 7 and 8 only on a view, and a view only with 7 or 8. They are not holey and get no FactSnapshot name yet: `elements::to_fact` gives none for them, so the "FactSnapshot kind list" map is unchanged (the 2026-11-10 Change-log row says why). 9-15 stay refused. Their kinds-set bits 17 and 18 are inside the IC_LAYOUT 4 mask, and the Spike C kind guard exits on them (`vm_layout::ARRAY_KINDS` holds 0-5) | None | An append is a fill (README §3.4 "Append-only parts"); an existing value never changes; G8 never counts an append. Each value has its keyed kinds-set bit (bit 10 + value), an IC_LAYOUT fact: IC_LAYOUT 4 holds bits 10-25 for all 16 values (H16 "Fact meanings"; M6-PLAN-D4), so an append no longer moves IC_LAYOUT (under IC_LAYOUT 3, bits 10-16, the first append would have, M5-P5-ORCH-10) | `tests::layout5::elements_kind_values_and_storage_forms` (the values as built); `tests::layout5::h18_kind_value_probe_15` (kind 15, the top of the 4 shape bits, never the next free value 7); `tests::elements::the_lattice_is_one_way` (`crates/ep9_vm/src/tests/elements.rs:120-123`), the IC_LAYOUT guard: it pins the pair (`IC_LAYOUT_VERSION`, kinds-set mask) = (4, `0xFFFF << 10`), so a change of the mask fails unless IC_LAYOUT moves with it; `tests::layout9::elements_kinds_7_and_8` (the values 7 and 8, their storage form, their kinds-set bits, and an Array that holds one fails `storage_form_ok`) [2026-11-10, TypedArray TA-0 TA-DECL] |
| elements tuning (gated) | The T0 tuning numbers of elements: the dense limit 2^26 (`object::arr::MAX_LENGTH`); the gap that moves an Array to DICTIONARY (1,024, `elements::ARRAY_GAP`, V8's `kMaxGap`, est.); the dense growth max(i + 1, 2 x capacity, 4); the DICTIONARY bucket count (a power of two, at least 8, `elements::dict::MIN_BUCKETS`) and its growth. They are facts about T0, not rules for other tiers: no tier bakes them (MODES-CHECK-w20 MC-w20-8; the H19 T0 clause form, MC-w17-6) [2026-11-07, M7-P3 ELEMKINDS (route H18-TUNE, M6-ORCH-170):] Also the density check of the objects that are not Arrays (M7-P1 OBJ1, H18-Q2 Built text): `elements::DENSE_UNCHECKED` 5,000 (the cell size in slots up to which a dense store grows with no check; V8's `kMaxUncheckedFastElementsLength`, est.), `elements::SPARSE_FACTOR` 3 (a dense cell may take less than 3 times the words of an ELEMENTS_DICT cell of the same elements; V8's `kPreferFastElementsSizeFactor`, est.) and the recount bound of a refused store (`elements::REFUSED_RECOUNT`). The plain gap 1,024 is H17's (`elements::PLAIN_GAP`, "layout tuning (gated)") | A4 gate (M8 exit) | Frozen-part rules after the gate; before it a retune is a normal change, and G8 does not count it | BENCH-SET (plan v10 §1.1); `tests::elements::one_high_index_keeps_rss_flat` |
| extra element kinds | NONEXTENSIBLE, SEALED and FROZEN for an Array with elements (and later copy-on-write), one-way in that order, flags first (M6-PLAN-D5, M6-PLAN-D6): each is a shape `flags` bit (H17 "shape flag bits (append-only)"), not an elements kind value, as JSC keeps CopyOnWrite as a flag beside its indexing shape. The elements kind stays the storage form, so values 7-15 of "elements kind values (append-only)" stay for storage forms in the lattice (F7 typed storage, an undefined-in-double form). The build decides whether a DOUBLE or INT32 array keeps its storage form or first moves to ELEMENTS (the H18-Q5 proposal moved it, with kind values over ELEMENTS). Not built in month 6 (M6-PLAN-D6, v11in #8; no gate of the first milestone reads them and no Octane-7 or v8-v7 kernel freezes an array): until it is built, the shape's extensible bit holds non-extensibility, and a frozen or sealed Array with elements is DICTIONARY [2026-11-07, M7-P3 ELEMKINDS (plan v12 §6.2):] **Built, flags first, with no caller yet.** NONEXTENSIBLE is the shape's EXTENSIBLE bit clear; SEALED is shape flag bit 4 and FROZEN bit 5, always with SEALED (H17 "shape flag bits (append-only)"); one-way. A level sits only on the dictionary shape of a non-extensible Array with a dense kind (0-5). The build keeps the storage form: a DOUBLE or INT32 array keeps its kind and its cell, and a SEALED array's stores still move along the lattice. The element attributes are the level's (`elements::dense_attrs`: {w, e, c}, SEALED {w, e}, FROZEN {e}), and a FROZEN `length` is not writable (`elements::length_writable`). `elements::set_integrity` sets a level (SetIntegrityLevel for an Array, after `[[PreventExtensions]]`); the raw stores refuse what a level forbids (`object::array_set`: nothing into a FROZEN array and no hole into a SEALED one, `elements::store_kind`; `object::array_set_length`: a cut of a SEALED array over an element moves it to DICTIONARY first, `elements::level_cut`, and its non-configurable entries stop the cut at the last element), so a caller that gets false takes the attribute-aware path; a move to DICTIONARY (`elements::to_dictionary`) writes the level into each entry and a FROZEN `length` into the cell's flag, and drops the shape bits. The birth-kind rule (MODES-CHECK-w23 MC-w23-19): a level is never a birth form (a level shape is not extensible, so `literal_site::is_birth_shape` refuses it), and `elements::generalize_site` still stops at DICTIONARY, so 7-15 are no birth kind. No level path writes a size field (MC-w32-6); no cell kind is new. Until the readers that are outside the M7-P3 ELEMKINDS grant land (`exotic.rs` and `property.rs` read `elements::dense_attrs`, the StoreKeyed hit of `keyed_ic.rs` refuses a FROZEN array, the SetIntegrityLevel entry calls `elements::set_integrity`, the layout exports with OBJECT_LAYOUT 8), no code sets a level, so the as-built rule above still holds for every JS program [2026-11-08, M7-P3 integration fixer (the activation; M6-ORCH-242 (5)):] **The callers are built.** SetIntegrityLevel of an Array (`Object.freeze`, `Object.seal`; `ep9_builtins` `object.rs`) calls the new `Cx::set_elements_integrity` (`elements::set_integrity`) after `[[PreventExtensions]]`; `exotic::own_property` and `exotic::own_attrs` give `elements::dense_attrs`; the CreateDataProperty fast stores of `property.rs` (`define`, `define_keyed`, `define_index`) take an existing element only with no level; `ordinary_delete` and `delete_index` keep a SEALED or FROZEN element (false, or the TypeError of a strict delete); `ordinary_define_own_property` keeps a dense element in place for the level's own attributes; the StoreKeyed hit (`keyed_ic::fast_set`) sends a FROZEN array cold in each site state. So a frozen or sealed Array with dense elements keeps its dense kind, and the "until it is built" rule no longer holds. In the same commit (M6-ORCH-254; the rule 12 Spike C grant of M6-ORCH-256): the `ep9_vm::layout` exports `SHAPE_FLAGS` (56), `SHAPE_FLAG_SEALED` (16) and `SHAPE_FLAG_FROZEN` (32) with OBJECT_LAYOUT 7 -> 8 (rule 71), and the Spike C move: its `vm_layout` PINNED row at 8 and a level exit in `CheckArray` (a dense kind no longer means writable elements) | M2 exit | Frozen-part rules | [2026-11-07, M7-P3 ELEMKINDS:] UNIT(ep9_vm) `tests::elements` (each also under GC stress 1): `a_level_is_a_shape_flag_and_the_storage_form_stays`, `the_raw_stores_refuse_what_a_level_forbids`, `a_move_to_dictionary_keeps_the_level_in_each_entry`, `every_shape_made_from_a_level_shape_keeps_it`, `no_level_path_writes_a_size_field`, `a_level_never_becomes_a_birth_form`; `ep9_builtins` `tests/object.rs` `arrays_keep_each_integrity_level` (the JS lines, which pass in the DICTIONARY form and must pass in the flag form) [2026-11-08, M7-P3 integration fixer:] `tests::elements::the_readers_and_the_key_paths_follow_a_level`; `tests::layout6::the_object_layout_hand_fact_exports_hold_on_live_cells` (`SHAPE_FLAGS` and the level bits on a live sealed and a live frozen Array); `spikes/spike-c` `gate.sh` (the frozen-array store seeds); FASTDIFF `keyed-access` cases "a frozen or sealed array at a store site" and "a frozen array at a megamorphic store site" |
| FactSnapshot kind list | The FactSnapshot `ElementsKind` bytes (7 kinds, `crates/ep9_facts/src/bits.rs:195-223`) and the one map between them and the kind values (`elements::to_fact`; 7-15 map to none [2026-11-05, M6-P4 fixer (B-CHECK F7):] at M1 exit. This part freezes the form, not the count of 7: the fact byte of a named kind is its VM kind value (the identity), a value that is not named maps to none, and a name keeps its byte for good; which values are named follows "elements kind values (append-only)", where a new name is part of an append's fill), with the one month-6 `FACT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump (plan v9 V9-D10) that adds the extra element kinds and the typed array, arguments and string wrapper kinds. H16 owns the schema (FREEZE-M1 batch 1). **As built 2026-11-02 (M6-P0 SCHEMA6, FACT_SCHEMA 2; M6-PLAN-D5, D6, D10; MC-w23-8):** the `ElementsKind` bytes are the VM kind values of "elements kind values (append-only)" (PACKED_INT32 0 ... DICTIONARY 6; schema 1 put the holey kinds after the packed ones), so `elements::to_fact` is the identity on 0-6. The list is open: every elements-kind field (the shape's, a literal's, `Access::Element`) is a byte value + 1 (0 = none), and a value a reader does not name (7-15, or past them) reads as none for that field only, never a refusal of the snapshot; the keyed kinds set is a 16-bit open set (bit i = value i). An append is a fill: the kind names are outside the schema fingerprint. The extra element kinds add no value (they are shape flags, "extra element kinds"); the fact kind of a typed array, an arguments object or a string wrapper is the pair (`ShapeDesc::kind`, `ShapeDesc::elements`), with `ObjectKind` arguments 7 (written) and typed-array 8 (declared, no VM kind) and a string wrapper as primitive-wrapper 5 [2026-11-04, M6-P3 B1W; FREEZE-M1 B1 Check cell:] Checked against the as-built code at 39788b53: `FACT_SCHEMA_VERSION` 2 (`crates/ep9_facts/src/lib.rs`, `schema-history.txt` line 2); `schema.txt` lists the open list "ElementsKind u8: the H18 elements kind values 0-15; an unnamed value reads as none" and its names 0 packed-int32 to 6 dictionary in the VM order, outside the fingerprint; `elements::to_fact` is the identity on 0-6. H16 holds the schema (its "feedback core" part); this part holds the kind bytes and the map | M1 exit | Frozen-part rules | `tests::elements::the_fact_kind_map_is_one_to_one`; the SP-Facts round trip (Validation); the M6-P0 SCHEMA6 tests: `ep9_facts` `tests/encoding.rs` `an_unknown_open_value_drops_only_its_field` (an unnamed value reads as none for its field only, and the snapshot is kept), `shapes_that_fold_into_one_merge_their_cases`, `a_merge_after_a_fold_follows_the_ranks_of_the_folded_shapes` and `a_folded_shape_does_not_hide_the_order_of_its_neighbours` (an unnamed value that folds two shapes into one), `tests/schema.rs` `schema_history_pins_the_schema` (version 2); `ep9_vm` `tests::facts_export::shapes_name_their_elements_kind_and_arguments_objects_their_kind` and `keyed_records_show_receivers_bounds_holes_and_kinds` (the exporter writes the VM values); `ep9_cli` `tests/facts_out.rs` (the header says version 2); [2026-11-05, M6-P4 fixer (MC-P3-H18CHK, B-CHECK F6):] `ep9_facts` `tests/schema.rs` `open_list_names_stay_out_of_the_fingerprint_and_keep_their_numbers` (the kind bytes 0-6 keep their names) and `schema_text_matches_the_golden_file` (the `ElementsKind` and `ElementsKindSet` name lines of `schema.txt`; an append re-blesses it as part of its fill, "elements kind values (append-only)") (all pass at 39788b53) |

## Rules

| Rule | Check | From |
|---|---|---|
| v0: every element read tests for the hole value | `array_get` | M0 wk3 |
| Index keys never enter shape tables | `om10_an_index_key_on_a_non_array_makes_a_dictionary` (`tests/objects.rs`, one test per OM-10 trigger) | M0 wk4 |
| Kind transitions are one-way | unit tests per transition; a kind never returns to a narrower kind: `tests::elements` `the_lattice_is_one_way`, `array_stores_make_one_way_transitions`, `every_kind_is_reached_and_never_left_downward`, `a_load_never_changes_a_kind` (each also under GC stress 1) | M1a (built M5-P1) |
| A DOUBLE element is a raw f64; the hole is one NaN pattern that NaN purification (H6) never makes | store and load tests with every NaN pattern: `tests::elements::every_nan_pattern_is_stored_canonical_and_never_as_the_hole` | M1a (built M5-P1) |
| A store far past the end goes to dictionary elements, not to a dense allocation | memory test: one high index keeps RSS flat: `tests::elements::one_high_index_keeps_rss_flat` (`a[2**32 - 2] = 1`, a `length` of 2^32 − 1 and `a[2**26 - 1] = 1` each take less than 1 MiB of heap, each under GC stress 1 too); `a_far_index_makes_a_dictionary_array`, `an_index_or_a_length_past_the_dense_limit_works` | M1a (built M5-P2) |
| The storage form follows the kind: the cell at +16 is the form the shape's kind names, so the shape guard is the kind source of every tier (M5-P1-ORCH-11; MODES-CHECK-w19 MC-w19-5) | `tests::elements::the_storage_form_follows_the_shape_kind` (every kind 0-6 reached by stores, defines and `length` writes; the check refuses a wrong form); `elements::storage_form_ok` asserted after each transition in debug builds; `elements::check` in the element tests | M1a (built M5-P2) |
| The feedback setter changes no shape and no elements kind (M-R9, MODES-D19): allocation-site kinds are IC data, not `profile.rs` records | `presets_object_model` (`crates/ep9_builtins/tests/`): one workload under `full` and `ics_only` on the jitless preset gives equal literal facts, shape sets and kind counts | M1a (built M5-P2; PROFILE6 re-runs it in M5-P3) |

## Consequences

- **Gains (target).** No tag check per element in numeric arrays. A kind is one byte that ICs and FactSnapshot can carry.
- **Costs (target).** More IC states (one per kind) and kind transitions in every store path. A NaN payload is reserved for the hole.
- **Costs (v0).** Holes and every element cost 8 bytes. A high index allocates the dense range. Indices at or above 2^26 break `length`. Files that use indices near 2^32 cannot pass until dictionary elements exist (59 `built-ins/Array` files name 4294967294 or 4294967295).
- **Follow-ups.** Dictionary elements remove the 2^26 limit (H18-Q4; built M5-P2). H17 decides where non-array objects keep elements (H18-Q3). NONEXTENSIBLE, SEALED and FROZEN (H18-Q5) are not built in month 5 (V10-D8): today the shape's extensible bit holds non-extensibility, and `Object.freeze` or `Object.seal` of an Array with elements moves it to DICTIONARY (each element's new attributes are an entry); an empty one keeps its kind.

| Tag | Claim used here | Link |

| [R06] | V8 stores elements apart from properties, in 21 elements kinds: a one-way lattice of PACKED/HOLEY over SMI, DOUBLE and ELEMENTS | [06-execution-tiers.md](../research/06-execution-tiers.md) |
| [M] | v0 limits and the high-index RSS above | This ADR, measured 2026-10-21 |

## Validation

- M1a: unit tests per kind and transition; test262 `built-ins/Array` with no new failure; the elements kind appears in H16 feedback.
- SP-Facts (M1): the FactSnapshot elements kind round-trips.

## Open questions

| ID | Question | Needed by | Proposed default |
|---|---|---|---|
| H18-Q1 | The hole NaN bit pattern for DOUBLE, next to the canonical NaN (H06-Q3) | M1a | A NaN payload that purification never makes. **Proposal 2026-10-27 (M4-P2 ADR-A0, Draft; the month-5 ELEMENTS-KINDS input):** the hole is 0xFFF7_FFFF_FFF7_FFFF (V8's hole NaN, external). Every DOUBLE-elements store, in every tier, makes a NaN canonical first (`PURE_NAN_BITS` 0x7FF8_0000_0000_0000, H06-Q3, `crates/ep9_value/src/value.rs:39`), so it never writes the hole; boxing already does this for T0, but an unboxed f64 from JIT or unit code (for example a `Float64Array` element whose bits are the hole pattern) must be made canonical by the store itself; a load compares all 64 bits. The DOUBLE store is a raw f64 cell, a new kind with no Values (OBJECT_LAYOUT 5 in M5-P0, V9-D39), so the GC never reads its words (H21 P1). **Built (M5-P1 ELEM1):** as proposed for T0 (`object::double_bits`, `object::double_value`; M5-P1 notes) **Decided (2026-11-01, M5-P4 FREEZE-PREP):** as built: the hole is 0xFFF7_FFFF_FFF7_FFFF, every DOUBLE store in every tier writes a NaN as the canonical NaN, and a load compares all 64 bits. In elements core |
| H18-Q2 | The sparse rule: when does a store go to dictionary elements? | M1a | A gap rule on (index − length) and a fill ratio, as V8. **Proposal 2026-10-27 (M4-P2 ADR-A0, Draft):** a store at index i goes to DICTIONARY when i − capacity ≥ 1,024 (V8's `kMaxGap`, external; est.), or when the new capacity would pass the dense limit 2^26; a `length` set past the dense limit also goes to DICTIONARY. The move is one-way (the Decision lattice); no fill-ratio rule moves an array back to a dense kind in v1. Dictionary entries hold (value or ACCESSOR_PAIR, attributes), so element accessors and non-default attributes use it too (H17 Element accessors row) **Built (M5-P2 ELEM2):** as proposed for Arrays: a new element 1,024 or more past the capacity (`elements::ARRAY_GAP`) or at or past 2^26, a `length` past 2^26, non-default attributes, an accessor element and a non-writable `length` move an Array to DICTIONARY, one-way (M5-P2 notes). Other JS object kinds keep the H18-Q3 rule (such an element leaves +16 for the property storage) **Decided (2026-11-01, M5-P4 FREEZE-PREP):** as built for Arrays: the gap rule, the dense-limit rule and the attribute rules move an Array to DICTIONARY, one-way, with no fill-ratio rule back; other JS object kinds keep the H18-Q3 rule. The rules are in elements core; the numbers (1,024 and 2^26) are in elements tuning **Built (M7-P1 OBJ1, 2026-11-06; route OBJ-FARKEY, RTS FE-3, V12-D27; for Arrays taken back by fixer r2b, M6-ORCH-173):** for the JS objects that are not Arrays (the H18-Q3 rule), a density check joins the gap rule, as V8 does for every JSObject (`ShouldConvertToSlowElements`, external): a new element at or past the capacity does not grow the dense store when it is 1,024 or more past it (as before), or when the grown cell would pass 5,000 slots (`elements::DENSE_UNCHECKED`, V8's `kMaxUncheckedFastElementsLength`, est.) and take 3 times (`elements::SPARSE_FACTOR`, V8's `kPreferFastElementsSizeFactor`, est.) or more the words of an ELEMENTS_DICT cell for its present elements and the new one (`elements::sparse_growth`); the object then keeps the index as a stored property (H18-Q3). Arrays keep the Decided rule above, with no density check (route ARRAY-DENSITY). The bit-flag enum form `o[1 << k] = v` (k = 0..27) on a plain object, whose stores land at the capacity so the gap rule never fired, now keeps a dense store of at most 5,000 slots, not 2^28 (`tests::elements::the_flag_enum_form_stays_small_on_a_plain_object`; `crates/ep9_builtins/tests/far_keys.rs`: 229,568 B of heap for the script at the fixer r2b head). The rule is in elements core; 5,000 and 3 are T0 tuning numbers, as the gap is. No fill-ratio rule moves a store back to a dense kind |
| H18-Q3 | Where do non-array objects keep elements? | M1a | The +16 ELEMENTS slot of the H17 v0 layout. **Proposal 2026-10-27 (M4-P2 ADR-A0, Draft): as proposed, for every JS object kind.** The elements kind lives in the shape, so one shape compare guards it; an array-index key on a non-array goes to +16 and stops being an OM-10 trigger (H17 Elements for every kind row). ARGUMENTS already keeps its elements at +16; string wrappers and module namespaces keep their exotic hooks; typed arrays are their own kinds with off-heap data (H25). **Built (M5-P1 ELEM1), with one change:** every JS object kind but Array (its own paths), PRIMITIVE_WRAPPER and the routed kinds keeps {w, e, c} index keys at +16, always holey (no `length`). An int32 key makes the cell; a string index key (`{0: v}`, `Object.defineProperty(o, "0", d)`) goes there only once the object has a cell, else it stays an OM-10 trigger as in v0, because the index test of a string key is a call that every named define would pay (BENCH-SET ci-v0). An accessor, other attributes or an index 1,024 or more past the capacity stay stored properties; own keys merge both stores in ascending order (test `plain_objects_keep_index_keys_in_elements`) **Decided (2026-11-01, M5-P4 FREEZE-PREP):** as built (M5-P1 ELEM1, with its one change). In elements core |
| H18-Q4 | When do the v0 limits go (2^26 cap, `length` past it)? | M1 | With dictionary elements. **Proposal 2026-10-27 (M4-P2 ADR-A0, Draft): with DICTIONARY elements in month 5 (ELEMENTS-KINDS).** 2^26 stays the dense limit, but an index or a `length` past it goes to DICTIONARY (Q2) instead of a string key or a RangeError, so `length` reaches 2^32 − 1 (ECMA-262) and the 59 `built-ins/Array` files that name 4294967294 or 4294967295 can pass **Built (M5-P2 ELEM2):** as proposed; ARRAY +24 holds a double Value for a `length` from 2^31 on (M5-P2 notes) **Decided (2026-11-01, M5-P4 FREEZE-PREP):** as built: 2^26 stays the dense limit, an index or a `length` past it is DICTIONARY, and `length` reaches 2^32 − 1. The rule is in elements core; 2^26 is in elements tuning |
| H18-Q5 | Frozen, sealed and non-extensible arrays | M1a | Separate kinds (06 §2.3). **Proposal 2026-10-27 (M4-P2 ADR-A0, Draft):** the kinds NONEXTENSIBLE, SEALED and FROZEN over ELEMENTS, each packed or holey, one-way in that order (V8 has the same six, external); a DOUBLE or INT32 array first moves to ELEMENTS. The FactSnapshot `ElementsKind` byte list (7 kinds, `crates/ep9_facts/src/bits.rs:207-222`) gets them, with the typed array, arguments and string wrapper kinds, in the one month-6 `FACT_SCHEMA_VERSION` bump (V9-D10) **Not built in month 5 (V10-D8, time):** the three kinds and their FactSnapshot bytes stay the append-only "extra element kinds" part of the freeze (kinds 7-15 of the 4 shape bits are reserved, `elements::to_fact` maps them to none); until then the shape's extensible bit holds non-extensibility, and a frozen or sealed Array with elements is DICTIONARY (its element attributes are entries) **Decided (2026-11-01, M5-P4 FREEZE-PREP):** as proposed, built later (V10-D8): the three kinds are the "extra element kinds" part and their FactSnapshot bytes the "FactSnapshot kind list" part, both due at M1 exit (plan v10 V10-A2; "extra element kinds" is not an append-only part, though the "Not built in month 5" text above called it one). Their kind values sit in the part "elements kind values (append-only)" (freeze point None; M5-P5-ORCH-1): the values as built stay, and each new kind takes the next free value of 7-15. Until they are built, the as-built rule above holds; it is in elements core |

## Reopens if

- SP-Facts or Spike C cannot express an elements kind in FactSnapshot (H16) or in deopt metadata (H29).
- The M2 peak-RSS cap (G7) fails because of element storage.

(1) In H18 it changed only the H18-Q1 Proposal text and added this row. The fix is right: boxing makes a NaN canonical (`ep9_value` `PURE_NAN_BITS` 0x7FF8_0000_0000_0000), but an unboxed f64 store can carry the hole bits, so the store must canonicalize. At 328137c5 every DOUBLE store does: the T0 store (`object::double_bits` maps every NaN to `delems::CANONICAL_NAN`) and both Spike C (Mode::Jit) store paths (`emit_a64` the Float64 value and `double_form` the Tagged value: `fcmp d16, d16` / `b.vc`, else `PURE_NAN_BITS`, the same bits) write every NaN as the canonical NaN, so no store writes the hole bits; no unit (AOT) code stores DOUBLE elements yet. (2) Kind test (README §3.3, quoted): clarify "Text only. No check, golden file, test or value changes result. The PR changes no code"; fill "Adds a rule or value where the ADR had an Open question or a part the plan defers. The claim (every DOUBLE store writes a NaN as the canonical NaN, never the hole), read at HEAD on all three store paths: (1) T0: `object.rs` lines 1004-1010 are `double_bits` (a NaN stores `CANONICAL_NAN`, line 325); each T0 writer of DOUBLE data writes `double_bits(v)` (`keyed_ic.rs` line 194, `literal_site.rs` lines 129 and 175, `object.rs` lines 1215 and 1308, `elements.rs` `to_doubles`), the hole only for a hole, or copies bits already stored (`object.rs` line 1292, growth). (2) Spike C `emit_a64/mod.rs` lines 1023-1037, a Float64 value: `fmov d16`, `fcmp d16, d16`, `b.vc` to the store, else `mov64` `PURE_NAN_BITS`, then `str` to the DOUBLE data; lines 1039-1045, any other unboxed value (Int32 or Bool): `scvtf` of the low 32 bits (never a NaN). (3) Spike C `double_form` (lines 1616-1654), a Tagged value: an int32 converts by `scvtf`; a double is decoded and checked the same way (`PURE_NAN_BITS` at line 1649, `str` at line 1653); any other Value goes to the slow path, the T0 store. `ep9_value` `value.rs` line 39 `PURE_NAN_BITS` is 0x7FF8_0000_0000_0000, the same bits as `CANONICAL_NAN`; the hole 0xFFF7_FFFF_FFF7_FFFF (`object.rs` line 323) differs, and Spike C `vm_layout.rs` line 421 checks that. `ir/mod.rs` line 456: `Graph::new` sets `mode: Mode::Jit`. No unit (AOT) code stores DOUBLE elements. spike-c `double_stores_and_the_kind_transition_equal_t0` and `a_tagged_copy_stores_every_value_form` pass at a42d69db (they store NaN through the Float64 and Tagged paths and compare the output with T0; they do not read the stored bits). `git show --stat a2e2d9e`: docs only (15 ADR and plan files, H18 +2 -1). True. Kind clarify: agrees with row 1.
